## 2.1 life ledger of pieces (children <= 3 at birth), r500
net = (own-eaten death pearls, or final length if alive) + mass split off to children - birth length. new mass = natural + enemy-drop pearls eaten - sprint cost - (length at death - own-eaten death pearls).

## 2.1 life ledger of pieces (children <= 3 at birth), elim
net = (own-eaten death pearls, or final length if alive) + mass split off to children - birth length. new mass = natural + enemy-drop pearls eaten - sprint cost - (length at death - own-eaten death pearls).

vor = own-territory spawn capacity where our nearest head (static path) is nearer than theirs (tie 1/2); unc = own capacity with no head of either team within 6; ecl = own capacity where their head is nearer; svc = capacity-weighted mean distance from own capacity to our nearest head (cap 30); cellP = mean expected spawns per 30 rounds in a piece's Voronoi cell (all heads).

## 5. material through each link, pearls per team-game (phase groups by the round of the event; pieces by birth round)
conv-like = pieces (<=4, not stubs) sacrificed with an own head >= 8 within Manhattan 3, pearls eaten by own dragons >= 8; piece->piece = pieces sacrificed with only shorter own heads near (S), pearls eaten by own; mid/big sac = sacrifices of dragons >= 5, pearls eaten by own >= 8; recycling = trapped pieces that are not stubs, pearls eaten by own; stub = a <=4 remnant of its own split within 2 rounds (U-turn / escape-split loop), any cause, pearls eaten by own; grab own / enemy = death drops within 6 of our heads eaten by us; forage = natural pearls eaten by pieces born in the phase group.
